\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)-\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:compressor_contraction}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}+\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{g_{i}}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{g_{i}}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+{2(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu^{-1})}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+2(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu^{-1}){\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle+{(1+s^{-1})}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right].

To further bound the last term, which contains multiple
{(1+s^{-1})}
factors, we leverage the property that
\hat{g}_{i}(x^{t+1})
is a random variable serving as an unbiased estimator of
\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})
, taking the form
\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})=\dfrac{1}{\tau_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}\nabla f_{\xi_{ij}^{t}}(x^{t+1})\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}
where
\xi_{ij}^{t}
are independent random variables. Next, we can continue as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{1}{\tau_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}\nabla f_{\xi_{ij}^{t}}(x^{t+1})-\dfrac{1}{\tau_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}{\nabla f_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}\left(\nabla f_{\xi_{ij}^{t}}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right)\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}{\tau_{i}}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}\Big(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f_{\xi_{ij}^{t}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla f_{\xi_{ij}^{t}}(x^{t+1})\;|\;W^{t}\right]\right\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}{\tau_{i}}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{\tau_{i}}\Big(2A_{i}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+B_{i}\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\|^{2}+C_{i}-\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}\right\|^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2A_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{2(B_{i}-1){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2A_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{2(B_{i}-1){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}L_{i}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}.

Furthermore, as a result of leveraging Assumption
2
, we can derive the subsequent bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})G_{i}^{t}+\dfrac{\hat{\beta_{1}}L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}.

Applying the tower property and subsequently taking the expectation, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}L_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

(3.47)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}.

Regarding the expectation of
G^{t+1}
, we derive the subsequent bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:weighted_ef21_sgd_aux_1}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}\tau_{i}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{n^{2}w_{i}\tau_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{n^{2}w_{i}\tau_{i}}.

Employing quantities
\tilde{A}
and
\tilde{C}
, the final bound can be reformulated as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}.

Given that
w_{i}=\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{\sum_{j}L_{j}}{n}L_{i}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{i}\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathchar 59\relax

what completes the proof.
∎

E3.3
Main result

Now we are ready to prove the main convergence theorem.

Theorem 13
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{C}(\alpha)
for all
\in[n]
and
t\geq 0
in Algorithm
4
. Set the following quantities:

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 1-\left(1-\alpha\right)(1+s)(1+\nu)\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 2(1-\alpha)\left(1+s\right)\left(s+\nu^{-1}\right)\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 2(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu^{-1})+(1+s^{-1})\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle w_{i}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\left(\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1))}{\tau_{i}}\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\right)\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\left(\dfrac{C_{i}}{\tau_{i}}\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\right).

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
, and
5
, and selection of
s>0
,
\mu>0
such that

(1+s)(1+\mu)<\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha}

set the step size in the following way:

\gamma\leq\dfrac{1}{L+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\hat{\beta}_{1}}{\hat{\theta}}}}.

(3.48)

Choose an iterate
\hat{x}^{T}
from
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax x^{1}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
with probability

\mathbf{Prob}(\hat{x}^{T}=x^{t})=\dfrac{v_{t}}{V_{T}}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

(3.49)

where

v_{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\tilde{A}\tilde{\beta}_{2}}{2\theta}\right)^{t};\qquad V_{T}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}v_{t}.

Then,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{2(f(x^{0})-f^{\text{inf}})}{\gamma T\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta}\right)^{T}}+\dfrac{G^{0}}{\hat{\theta}T\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta}\right)^{T}}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.50)

where

G^{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\|g_{i}^{0}-\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\|^{2}.

Proof.

In the derivation below, we use Lemma
3
for

g^{t}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}g_{i}^{t}.

(3.51)

We start as follows:

\displaystyle f(x^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:descent_lemma}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

(3.52)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|g^{t}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sgd_weighted_grad_estimate_def}}{=}

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}G^{t}.

Subtracting
f^{\ast}
from both sides and taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\ast}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

(3.53)

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right].

Let

\displaystyle\delta^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle s^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle r^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then by adding
\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta}s^{t+1}
and employing (
3.46
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)r^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}s^{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}\left(\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}r^{t}+(1-\hat{\theta})s^{t}+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\delta^{t+1}+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}\right)r^{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

The last inequality follows from the bound

\gamma^{2}\dfrac{\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}}{\hat{\theta}}+L\gamma\leq 1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

which holds due to Lemma
5
for

\gamma\leq\dfrac{1}{L+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\hat{\beta}_{1}}{\hat{\theta}}}}.

Subsequently, we will reconfigure the final inequality and perform algebraic manipulations, taking into account that
\dfrac{2}{\gamma}>0.
In the final step of these algebraic transformations, we will leverage the fact that
s^{t}\geq 0
:

\displaystyle\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

Further,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-1\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\delta^{t+1}-\left(\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}.

Next, we sum up inequalities above with weights
v_{t}/V_{T}
, where

v_{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{t}\quad\mathrm{and}\quad V_{T}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{T}v_{i}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\dfrac{v_{t}}{V_{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}v_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}v_{t}\Big(\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}w_{t}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\dfrac{w_{t}}{W_{T}}\cdot\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}w_{t}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(w_{t}\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-w_{t+1}\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2\delta^{0}}{\gamma V_{T}}+\dfrac{s^{0}}{\hat{\theta}V_{T}}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}.

Finally, we notice that

V_{T}=\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{t}\geq T\cdot(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{T}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

what concludes the proof.
∎

F3
EF21-W-SGD: Weighted Error Feedback 2021 with Stochastic Gradients under the ABC Assumption

In this section, we present the convergence result for
Weighted EF21
in the setting where the gradient computation on the clients is replaced with a pretty general unbiased stochastic gradient estimator.

F3.1
Algorithm

The
EF21-W
algorithm assumes that all clients can compute the exact gradient in each round. In some scenarios, the exact gradients may be unavailable or too costly to compute, and only approximate gradient estimators can be obtained. To have the ability for
EF21-W
to work in such circumstances we extended
EF21-W
to handle stochastic gradients. We called the resulting algorithm
EF21-W-SGD
(Algorithm
5
).

1:

Input:
initial model
x^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
; initial gradient estimates
g_{1}^{0}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax g_{n}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
stored at the server and the clients; step size
\gamma>0
; number of iterations
T>0
; weights
{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}=\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}
for
i\in[n]

2:

Initialize:

g^{0}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}g_{i}^{0}
on the server

3:

for

t=0\mathchar 59\relax 1\mathchar 59\relax 2\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax T-1

do

4:

Server computes
x^{t+1}=x^{t}-\gamma g^{t}
and broadcasts
x^{t+1}
to all
n
clients

5:

for

i=1\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax n

on the clients in parallel

do

6:

Compute a stochastic gradient
\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})
estimator of the gradient
\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})

7:

Compute
u_{i}^{t}=\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{1}{n{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}}\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})-g_{i}^{t}\right)
and update
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}

8:

Send the compressed message
u_{i}^{t}
to the server

9:

end

for

10:

Server updates
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}
for all
i\in[n]
, and computes
g^{t+1}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}g_{i}^{t+1}

11:

end

for

12:

Output:
Point
\hat{x}^{T}
chosen from the set
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
randomly according to the law (
3.59
)

Algorithm 5

EF21-W-SGD
: Weighted EF-21 with Stochastic Gradients under ABC assumption.

Our analysis of this extension follows a similar approach as the one used by
Fatkhullin et al. 2021
for studying the stochastic gradient version under the name
EF21-SGD
. However,
EF21-W-SGD
has four important differences with vanilla
EF21-SGD
:

1.

Vanilla
EF21-SGD
algorithm analyzed by
Fatkhullin et al. 2021
worked under a specific sampling schema for a stochastic gradient estimator. Our analysis works under a more general ABC Assumption
6
.

2.

Vanilla
EF21-SGD
provides maximum theoretically possible

\gamma=\left(L+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}L_{\rm QM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\beta_{1}}{\theta}}\right)^{-1}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

where
EF21-W-SGD
has

\gamma=\left(L+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\beta_{1}}{\theta}}\right)^{-1}.

3.

In contrast to the original analysis
Vanilla EF21-SGD
our analysis provides a more aggressive
\beta_{1}
parameter which is smaller by a factor of
2
.

4.

Vanilla
EF21-SGD
and
EF21-W-SGD
formally differs in a way how it reports iterate
x^{T}
which minimizes
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]
due to a slightly different definition of
\widetilde{A}
. The
EF21-W-SGD
(Algorithm
5
) requires output iterate
\hat{x}^{T}
randomly according to the probability mass function described by Equation (
3.59
).

Assumption 6

(General assumption for stochastic gradient estimators)
.

We assume that for all
i\in[n]
there exist parameters
A_{i}\mathchar 59\relax C_{i}\geq 0
,
B_{i}\geq 1
such that

\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla\hat{g_{i}}(x)\|^{2}\right]\leq 2A_{i}\left(f_{i}(x)-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+B_{i}\|\nabla f_{i}(x)\|^{2}+C_{i}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.54)

holds for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
where
4
4

4

When
A_{i}=0
one can ignore the first term in the right-hand side of (
3.54
), i.e., assumption
\inf_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{i}(x)>-\infty
is not required in this case.

f_{i}^{\inf}=\inf_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{i}(x)>-\infty
.

Assumption 7

(Unbiased assumption for stochastic gradient estimators)
.

We assume that for all
i\in[n]
there following holds for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
:

\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{g_{i}}(x)\right]=\nabla f_{{i}}(x).

We study
EF21-W-SGD
under Assumption
6
and Assumption
7
.To the best of our knowledge, this Assumption
6
, which was originally presented as Assumption 2 by
Khaled and Richtárik 2023
, is the most general assumption for a stochastic gradient estimator in a non-convex setting. For a detailed explanation of the generality of this assumption see Figure 1 of
Khaled and Richtárik 2023
.

F3.2
A lemma

The contraction Lemma in this case gets the following form:

Lemma 11
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{C}(\alpha)
for all
i\in[n]
and
t\geq 0
. Define

G_{i}^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\mathchar 59\relax\qquad G^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}G_{i}^{t}.

Let Assumptions
2
,
6
,
7
hold. Then, for any
s>0\mathchar 59\relax\nu>0
during execution of the Algorithm
5
the following holds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]

(3.55)

\displaystyle+\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]

\displaystyle+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathchar 59\relax

where

\displaystyle w_{i}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 1-\left(1-\alpha\right)(1+s)(1+\nu)

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)\left(1+s\right)\left(s+\nu^{-1}\right)\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)+(1+s^{-1})\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\left({2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1))}\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\right)\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\left({C_{i}}\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\right).

Proof.

Define
W^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{g_{1}^{t}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax g_{n}^{t}\mathchar 59\relax x^{t}\mathchar 59\relax x^{t+1}\}
. The proof starts as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:weighted_def_grad_distortion}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g_{i}^{t+1}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{line}~\ref{ch3:line:weighted_ef21_sgd_grad_update_abc}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g_{i}^{t}+\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)-\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)+\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)-\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:compressor_contraction}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|\left(\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{g_{i}}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|{g_{i}}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}|\;W^{t}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1-\alpha)(1+s)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+s^{-1})\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right].

Further, we continue as follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{g_{i}}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha)(1+s)(1+\nu^{-1}){\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle+{\left((1+s^{-1})+(1-\alpha)(1+s)\right)}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\dfrac{\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right].

To further bound the last term, which contains multiple
{(1+s^{-1})}
factors, we leverage the property that
\hat{g}_{i}(x^{t+1})
is a random variable serving as an unbiased estimator of
\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})
. Our approach is as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right].

Now due to the requirement of unbiasedness of gradient estimators expressed in the form of Assumption
7
we have the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

(3.56)

\displaystyle\qquad-{\left\|\nabla f_{{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}}

Using this variance decomposition, we can proceed as follows.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:var-decompoposition-abc}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{g_{i}}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\right]-{\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\right\|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:general_second_mom_upp_bound_abc}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\Big(2A_{i}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+B_{i}\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C_{i}-\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1}\right\|^{2})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2A_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(B_{i}-1){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(\dfrac{1}{2}\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})\|^{2}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2A_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(B_{i}-1){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}L_{i}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\;|\;W^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}.

Next leveraging Assumption
2
we replace the second term in the last expression, and we can derive the subsequent bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:L_i}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})G_{i}^{t}+\dfrac{\hat{\beta_{1}}L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\left(f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right)+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}.

Applying the tower property and subsequently taking the expectation, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}L_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

(3.57)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]+\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}.

Next for the expectation of the main quantity of our interest
G^{t+1}
, we derive the subsequent bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:weighted_ef21_sgd_aux_1_abc}}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(nw_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1)){\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{(n)^{2}w_{i}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{C_{i}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}{n^{2}w_{i}}

Employing quantities
\tilde{A}
and
\tilde{C}
, the final bound can be reformulated as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f_{i}^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[f_{i}(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}.

Given that
w_{i}=\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\beta_{1}}\dfrac{\sum_{j}L_{j}}{n}L_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}

\displaystyle=(1-\hat{\theta})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\hat{\beta_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{i}\right)^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\inf}\right]+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\mathchar 59\relax

what completes the proof.
∎

F3.3
Main result

Now we are ready to prove the main convergence theorem.

Theorem 14
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{C}(\alpha)
for all
\in[n]
and
t\geq 0
in Algorithm
5
. set the following quantities:

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 1-\left(1-\alpha\right)(1+s)(1+\nu)\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)\left(1+s\right)\left(s+\nu^{-1}\right)\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\hat{\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s)+(1+s^{-1})\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle w_{i}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}L_{j}}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\dfrac{2(A_{i}+L_{i}(B_{i}-1))}{nw_{i}}\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle\widetilde{C}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\max_{i=1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\ldots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}n}\dfrac{C_{i}}{nw_{i}}.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
6
,
7
, and selection of
s>0\mathchar 59\relax\nu>0
small enough such that

(1+s)(1+\nu)<\dfrac{1}{1-\alpha}
holds, set the step size in the following way:

\gamma\leq\dfrac{1}{L+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\hat{\beta}_{1}}{\hat{\theta}}}}.

(3.58)

Choose an iterate
\hat{x}^{T}
from
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax x^{1}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
with probability

\mathbf{Prob}(\hat{x}^{T}=x^{t})=\dfrac{v_{t}}{V_{T}}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.59)

where

v_{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\tilde{A}\tilde{\beta}_{2}}{2\theta}\right)^{t};\qquad V_{T}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum\limits_{t=0}^{T-1}v_{t}.

Then,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{2(f(x^{0})-f^{\text{inf}})}{\gamma T\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta}\right)^{T}}+\dfrac{G^{0}}{\hat{\theta}T\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta}\right)^{T}}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.60)

where

G^{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|g_{i}^{0}-\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{0})\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

In the derivation below, we use Lemma
3
for

g^{t}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}g_{i}^{t}.

(3.61)

We start as follows:

\displaystyle f(x^{t+1})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:descent_lemma}}{\leq}

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|g^{t}-\dfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sgd_weighted_grad_estimate_def}}{=}

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left(g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f(x^{t})-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}G^{t}.

Subtracting
f^{\ast}
from both sides and taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\ast}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right].

Let

\displaystyle\delta^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle s^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

\displaystyle r^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then by adding
\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta}s^{t+1}
and employing inequality (
3.46
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)r^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}s^{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}\left(\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}r^{t}+(1-\hat{\theta})s^{t}+{\widetilde{A}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\delta^{t+1}+{\widetilde{C}{\hat{\beta_{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}\right)r^{t}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

The last inequality follows from the bound

\gamma^{2}\dfrac{\hat{\beta_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}}{\hat{\theta}}+L\gamma\leq 1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

which holds due to Lemma
5
for

\gamma\leq\dfrac{1}{L+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\sqrt{\dfrac{\hat{\beta}_{1}}{\hat{\theta}}}}.

Subsequently, we will reconfigure the final inequality and perform algebraic manipulations, taking into account that
\dfrac{2}{\gamma}>0
. In the final step of these algebraic transformations, we will leverage the fact that
s^{t}\geq 0
:

\displaystyle\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}.

Further,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{C}}{2\hat{\theta}}\beta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\delta^{t+1}-\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)+\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-1\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\delta^{t+1}-\left(\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}.

We sum up inequalities above with weights
v_{t}/V_{T}
, where

v_{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{t}\mathchar 59\relax\qquad V_{T}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{T}v_{i}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\dfrac{v_{t}}{V_{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}v_{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}v_{t}\Big(\dfrac{2}{\gamma}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}w_{t}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\dfrac{w_{t}}{W_{T}}\cdot\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}w_{t}\left(\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-\left(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\beta_{2}}{2\hat{\theta}}\right)\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma V_{T}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(w_{t}\left(\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t}\right)-w_{t+1}\left(\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\hat{\theta}}s^{t+1}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2\delta^{0}}{\gamma V_{T}}+\dfrac{s^{0}}{\hat{\theta}V_{T}}+\dfrac{\widetilde{C}\beta_{2}}{\hat{\theta}}.

Finally, we notice that

V_{T}=\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{t}\geq T\cdot(1-\dfrac{\gamma\widetilde{A}\hat{\beta_{2}}}{2\theta})^{T}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

what concludes the proof.
∎

G3
EF21-W-PP: Weighted Error Feedback 2021 with Partial Participation

In this section, we present another extension of error feedback. Again, to maintain brevity, we show our results for
EF21-W
, however, we believe getting an enhanced rate for standard
EF21
should be straightforward.

G3.1
Algorithm

Building upon the delineation of
EF21-W
in Algorithm
3
, we turn our attention to its partial participation variant,
EF21-W-PP
, and seek to highlight the primary distinctions between them. One salient difference is the introduction of a distribution, denoted as
\mathcal{D}
, across the clients. For clarity, consider the power set
{\cal P}
of the set
[n]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{1\mathchar 59\relax 2\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax n\}
, representing all possible subsets of
[n]
. Then, the distribution
{\cal D}
serves as a discrete distribution over
{\cal P}
.

While
EF21-W-PP
runs, at the start of each communication round
t
, the master, having computed a descent step as
x^{t+1}=x^{t}-\gamma g^{t}
, samples a client subset
S^{t}
from the distribution
\mathcal{D}
. Contrasting with Algorithm
3
where the new iteration
x^{t+1}
is sent to all clients, in this variant, it is sent exclusively to those in
S^{t}
.

Any client
i\in S^{t}
adheres to procedures akin to
EF21-W
: it compresses the quantity
\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})-g_{i}^{t}
and transmits this to the master. Conversely, client
j
omitted in
S^{t}
, i.e.,
j\notin S^{t}
, is excluded from the training for that iteration. Concluding the round, the master updates
g^{t+1}
by integrating the averaged compressed variances received from clients in the set
S^{t}
.

1:

Input:
initial model parameters
x^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
; initial gradient estimates
g_{1}^{0}\mathchar 59\relax g_{2}^{0}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}g_{n}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
stored at the clients; weights
{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}=\nicefrac{{L_{i}}}{{\sum_{j}L_{j}}}
; step size
\gamma>0
; number of iterations
T>0
; distribution
\mathcal{D}
over clients

2:

Initialize:

g^{0}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}g_{i}^{0}
on the server

3:

for

t=0\mathchar 59\relax 1\mathchar 59\relax 2\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax T-1

do

4:

Server computes
x^{t+1}=x^{t}-\gamma g^{t}

5:

Server samples a subset
S^{t}\sim\mathcal{D}
of clients

6:

Server broadcasts
x^{t+1}
to clients in
S^{t}

7:

for

i=1\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax n

on the clients in parallel

do

8:

if

i\in S^{t}

then

9:

Compute
u_{i}^{t}=\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}(\dfrac{1}{n{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-g_{i}^{t})
and update
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}

10:

Send the compressed message
u_{i}^{t}
to the server

11:

end

if

12:

if

i\notin S^{t}

then

13:

Set
u_{i}^{t}=0
for the client and the server

14:

Do not change local state
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}

15:

end

if

16:

end

for

17:

Server updates
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}
for all
i\in[n]
, and computes
g^{t+1}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.88,0}w_{i}}g_{i}^{t+1}

18:

end

for

19:

Output:
Point
\hat{x}^{T}
chosen from the set
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
uniformly at random

Algorithm 6

EF21-W-PP
: Weighted Error Feedback 2021 with Partial Participation.

Assume
S
is drawn from the distribution
\mathcal{D}
. Let us denote

p_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\mathbf{Prob}(i\in S^{t}).

(3.62)

In other words,
p_{i}
represents the probability of client
i
being selected in any iteration. For given parameters
p_{i}
such that
p_{i}\in(0\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}1]
for
i\in[n]
, we introduce the notations
p_{\min}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\min_{i}p_{i}
and
p_{\max}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\max_{i}p_{i}
, respectively.

G3.2
A lemma

Having established the necessary definitions, we can now proceed to formulate the Lemma.

Lemma 12
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{C}(\alpha)
for all
i\in[n]
and
t\geq 0
. Let Assumption
2
hold. Define

G_{i}^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\mathchar 59\relax\qquad G^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}G_{i}^{t}.

(3.63)

For any
s>0
and
\rho>0
, let us define the following quantities:

\displaystyle\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 1-(1-\alpha)(1+s)

\displaystyle\beta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\beta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)=(1-\alpha)(1+s^{-1})

\displaystyle\theta_{p}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle p_{\min}\rho+\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}-\rho-(p_{\max}-p_{\min})

\displaystyle\tilde{B}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}.

Additionally, assume that

\dfrac{1+\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}\geq\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)>\dfrac{\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}.

Then, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]\leq(1-\theta_{p})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\tilde{B}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

(3.64)

Proof.

Let us define
W^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{g_{1}^{t}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax g_{n}^{t}\mathchar 59\relax x^{t}\mathchar 59\relax x^{t+1}\}
. If client
i
participates in the training at iteration
t
, then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\in S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:grad_distortion_def_pp}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g_{i}^{t+1}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\in S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\text{line}~\ref{ch3:line:weighted_ef21_pp_grad_ind_update_step}\text{ of Algorithm~\ref{ch3:alg:weighted_ef21_pp}}}{=}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g_{i}^{t}+\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\left(\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right)-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}\;|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\in S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:compressor_contraction}}{\leq}(1-\alpha)\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}+\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}(1-\alpha)(1+s)\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha)\left(1+s^{-1}\right)\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:L_i}}{\leq}(1-\alpha)(1+s)\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-g_{i}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1-\alpha)\left(1+s^{-1}\right)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Utilizing the tower property and taking the expectation with respect to
W^{t}
, we derive:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\in S^{t}\right]\leq(1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\mathchar 59\relax

(3.65)

where
\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)=1-(1-\alpha)(1+s)
, and
\beta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)=(1-\alpha)(1+s^{-1})
. We now aim to bound the quantity
\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\notin S^{t}\right]
, starting with an application of the tower property:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\notin S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\notin S^{t}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:grad_distortion_def_pp}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|g_{i}^{t+1}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\notin S^{t}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\notin S^{t}\Big]\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:young}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}\Big[(1+\rho)\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+(1+\rho^{-1})\left\|\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})}{nw_{i}}-\dfrac{\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})}{nw_{i}}\right\|^{2}|\;W^{t}\mathchar 59\relax i\notin S^{t}\Big]\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+\rho)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\dfrac{(1+\rho^{-1})}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\;\right].

Given that Assumption
2
is satisfied, by applying (
3.7
) to the second term, we obtain:

\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\notin S^{t}\right]\leq(1+\rho)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}(1+\rho^{-1})}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

(3.66)

We combine the two preceding bounds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{Prob}(i\in S^{t})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\in S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{Prob}(i\notin S^{t})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\;|\;i\notin S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:p_i_def}}{=}

\displaystyle p_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}|\;i\in S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(1-p_{i})\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}|\;i\notin S^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:g_i_for_sampled_ef21_weighted}+\eqref{ch3:eq:g_i_for_non_sampled_ef21_weighted}}{\leq}

\displaystyle p_{i}\left[(1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\quad+(1-p_{i})\left[(1+\rho)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]+\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}(1+\rho^{-1})}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{i}+(1-p_{i})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{i}}+(1-p_{i}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Consequently, for
\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]
, we derive the subsequent bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:grad_distortion_def_pp}}{=}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{i}+(1-p_{i})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{i}}+(1-p_{i}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]\mathchar 59\relax

where we applied the preceding inequality. Remembering the definitions

p_{\min}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\min_{i}p_{i}\mathchar 59\relax\quad p_{\max}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\max_{i}p_{i}\mathchar 59\relax

we subsequently obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\mathbb{E}\left[G_{i}^{t}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}w_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Applying (
3.63
) and (
3.25
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{L_{i}^{2}}{n^{2}\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}L_{j}}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{n}\dfrac{L_{j}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{L_{i}}{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left((1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)\right)\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]

\displaystyle+\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Subsequently, in order to simplify the last inequality, we introduce the variables
1-\theta_{p}
and
\tilde{B}
:

\displaystyle 1-\theta_{p}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s))p_{\max}+(1-p_{\min})(1+\rho)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{\max}-p_{\max}\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)+1-p_{\min}+\rho-p_{\min}\rho

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\left(-p_{\max}+p_{\max}\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)+p_{\min}-\rho+p_{\min}\rho\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\left(p_{\min}\rho+p_{\max}\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)-\rho-(p_{\max}-p_{\min})\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\theta_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(p_{\max}\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)-\rho(1-p_{\min})-(p_{\max}-p_{\min})\right)

\displaystyle\tilde{B}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}.

Expressed in terms of these variables, the final inequality can be reformulated as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]\leq(1-\theta_{p})\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]+\tilde{B}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Since we need the contraction property over the gradient distortion
\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t+1}\right]
, we require
0<\theta_{p}\leq 1
. We rewrite these conditions as follows:

\dfrac{1+\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}\geq\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)>\dfrac{\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}.

∎

G3.3
Main result

We are ready to prove the main convergence theorem.

Theorem 15
.

Consider Algorithm
6
(
EF21-W-PP
) applied to the distributed optimization Problem
(
3.1
)
. Let Assumptions
1
,
2
,
3
hold, assume that
\mathcal{C}_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{C}(\alpha)
for all
i\in[n]
and
t>0
, set

G^{t}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|g_{i}^{t}-\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\mathchar 59\relax

where

w_{i}=\dfrac{L_{i}}{\sum_{j}L_{j}}\mathchar 59\relax\forall i\in[n]\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}

and let the step size satisfy

0<\gamma\leq\left(L+\sqrt{\dfrac{\tilde{B}}{\theta_{p}}}\right)^{-1}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.67)

where
s>0
,
\rho>0
, and

\displaystyle\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle 1-(1-\alpha)(1+s)

\displaystyle\beta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle(1-\alpha)(1+s^{-1})

\displaystyle\theta_{p}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle p_{\min}\rho+\theta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}-\rho-(p_{\max}-p_{\min})

\displaystyle\tilde{B}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\left({\beta(\alpha\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}s)p_{\max}}+(1-p_{\min}){(1+\rho^{-1})}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}.

Additionally, assume that

\dfrac{1+\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}\geq\theta(\alpha\mathchar 59\relax s)>\dfrac{\rho(1-p_{\min})+(p_{\max}-p_{\min})}{p_{\max}}.

If for
T>1
we define
\hat{x}^{T}
as an element of the set
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax x^{1}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
chosen uniformly at random, then

\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{2(f(x^{0})-f^{\ast})}{\gamma T}+\dfrac{G^{0}}{\theta_{p}T}.

(3.68)

Proof.

Following the same approach employed in the proof for the
SGD
case, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t+1})-f^{\ast}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right].

Let

\displaystyle\delta^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[f(x^{t})-f^{\ast}\right]\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle s^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[G^{t}\right]\mathchar 59\relax

\displaystyle r^{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{t+1}-x^{t}\right\|^{2}\right].

Applying the previous Lemma, we obtain:

\displaystyle\delta^{t+1}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}s^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}\right)r^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2}s^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}\left(\tilde{B}r^{t}+(1-\theta_{p})s^{t}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]-\underbrace{\left(\dfrac{1}{2\gamma}-\dfrac{L}{2}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}\tilde{B}\right)}_{\geq 0}r^{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{t}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}s^{t}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right].

By summing up inequalities for
t=0\mathchar 59\relax\ldots\mathchar 59\relax T-1\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}
we get

0\leq\delta^{T}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}s^{T}\leq\delta^{0}+\dfrac{\gamma}{2\theta_{p}}s^{0}-\dfrac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right].

Finally, via multiplying both sides by
\dfrac{2}{\gamma T}
, after rearranging we get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\dfrac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(x^{t})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{2\delta^{0}}{\gamma T}+\dfrac{s^{0}}{\theta_{p}T}.

It remains to notice that the left-hand side can be interpreted as
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla f(\hat{x}^{T})\right\|^{2}\right]
, where
\hat{x}^{T}
is chosen from the set
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax x^{1}\mathchar 59\relax\ldots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
uniformly at random.
∎

Our analysis of this extension follows a similar approach as the one used by
Fatkhullin et al. 2021
, for an algorithm they called
EF21-PP
. Presented analysis of
EF21-W-PP
has a better provides a better multiplicative factor in the definition of
\widetilde{B}\propto{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}^{2}}
, where in vanilla
EF21-PP
had
\widetilde{B}\propto{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}L_{\rm QM}^{2}}
. This fact improves the upper bound on allowable step size in (
3.67
).

H3
Improved Theory for EF21 in the Rare Features Regime

In this section, we adapt our new results to the
rare features
regime proposed and studied by
Richtárik et al. 2023
.

H3.1
Algorithm

In this section, we focus on Algorithm
7
, which is an adaptation of
EF21
(as delineated in Algorithm
2
) that specifically employs
TopK
operators. This variant is tailored for the rare features scenario, enhancing the convergence rate by shifting from the average of squared Lipschitz constants to the square of their average. The modifications introduced in Algorithm
7
, compared to the standard
EF21
, are twofold and significant.

Primarily, the algorithm exclusively engages
TopK
compressors, leveraging the inherent sparsity present in the data. Additionally, the initial gradient estimates
g_{i}^{0}
are confined to the respective subspaces
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
, as characterized by Equation (
3.71
). With the exception of these distinct aspects, the algorithm’s execution parallels that of the original
EF21
.

1:

Input:
initial model
x^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
; initial gradient estimates
g_{1}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{1}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\dots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}g_{n}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{n}
(as defined in Equation (
3.71
)) stored at the server and the clients; step size
\gamma>0
; sparsification levels
K_{1}\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}\dots\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}K_{n}\in[d]
; number of iterations
T>0

2:

Initialize:

g^{0}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}^{0}
on the server

3:

for

t=0\mathchar 59\relax 1\mathchar 59\relax 2\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax T-1

do

4:

Server computes
x^{t+1}=x^{t}-\gamma g^{t}
and broadcasts
x^{t+1}
to all
n
clients

5:

for

i=1\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax n

on the clients in parallel

do

6:

Compute
u_{i}^{t}=\text{Top}K_{i}(\nabla f_{i}(x^{t+1})-g_{i}^{t})
and update
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}

7:

Send the compressed message
u_{i}^{t}
to the server

8:

end

for

9:

Server updates
g_{i}^{t+1}=g_{i}^{t}+u_{i}^{t}
for all
i\in[n]
, and computes
g^{t+1}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}^{t+1}

10:

end

for

11:

Output:
Point
\hat{x}^{T}
chosen from the set
\{x^{0}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax x^{T-1}\}
uniformly at random

Algorithm 7

EF21
: Error Feedback 2021 with
TopK
Compressors.

H3.2
New sparsity measure

To extend our results to the
rare features
regime, we need to slightly change the definition of the parameter
c
in the original paper. The way we do it is unrolled as follows. First, we recall the following definitions from
(
Richtárik et al. 2023
)
:

\mathcal{Z}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\in[n]\times[d]\;|\;[\nabla f_{i}(x)]_{j}=0\ \forall x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.69)

and

{\cal I}_{j}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{i\in[n]\;|\;(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}\}\mathchar 59\relax\qquad\mathcal{J}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{j\in[d]\;|\;(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}\}.

(3.70)

We also need the following definition of
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
:

\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}\{u=(u_{1}\mathchar 59\relax\dots\mathchar 59\relax u_{d})\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:u_{j}=0\text{ whenever }(i\mathord{\mathchar 59\relax}j)\in\mathcal{Z}\}.

(3.71)

Now we are ready for a new definition of the sparsity parameter
c
:

c\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=}}n\cdot\max\limits_{j\in[d]}\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\mathchar 59\relax

(3.72)

where
w_{i}
is defined as in (
3.25
). We note that
c
recovers the standard definition from
(
Richtárik et al. 2023
)
when
w_{i}=\dfrac{1}{n}
for all
i\in[n]
.

H3.3
Lemmas

We will proceed through several Lemmas.

Lemma 13
.

Let
u_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
for all
i\in[n]
. Then, the following inequality holds:

\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{i}\right\|^{2}\leq\dfrac{c}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\|u_{i}\|^{2}.

(3.73)

Proof.

Initially, we observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{i}\right\|^{2}=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}\right)^{2}.

(3.74)

We note that for any
j\in[d]
it holds that

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}u_{ij}\right)^{2}

(3.75)

\displaystyle=\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\right)^{2}\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}\dfrac{w_{i}}{\sum\limits_{i^{\prime}\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i^{\prime}}}u_{ij}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\right)^{2}\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}\dfrac{w_{i}}{\sum\limits_{i^{\prime}\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i^{\prime}}}u_{ij}^{2}\mathchar 59\relax

where on the last line we used Jensen’s inequality. Subsequent arithmetic manipulations and the incorporation of definition (
3.72
) yield:

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_aux_2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\right)\cdot\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}u_{ij}^{2}

(3.76)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\right)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\max_{j\in[d]}\left(\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}w_{i}\right)\right]\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_c_definiton}}{=}

\displaystyle\dfrac{c}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}u_{ij}^{2}.

Substituting Equation (
3.76
) into Equation (
3.74
) completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 14
.

Assume that
g_{i}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
for all
i\in[n]
. Then, it holds for all
t>0
that

\|g^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}\leq\dfrac{c}{n}G^{t}.

(3.77)

Proof.

By Lemma 8 in
Richtárik et al. 2023
, for
EF21
the update
g_{i}^{t}
stays in
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
if
g_{i}^{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
. We then proceed as follows:

\displaystyle\|g^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}\overset{\eqref{ch3:line:sparse_averaging_line}}{=}\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\right\|^{2}=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left[\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}(g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))\right]\right\|^{2}.

(3.78)

Since
g_{i}^{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
, as was noted, and
\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
, by the definition of
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
, then
\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}(g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))
also belongs to
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{i}
. By Lemma
13
, we further proceed:

\displaystyle\|g^{t}-\nabla f(x^{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_aux_4}}{=}

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left[\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}(g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_tighter_bound}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\dfrac{c}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}\left\|\dfrac{1}{nw_{i}}(g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{c}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{n^{2}w_{i}}\|g_{i}^{t}-\nabla f_{i}(x^{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{c}{n}G^{t}\mathchar 59\relax

which completes the proof.
∎

For the convenience of the reader, we briefly revisit Lemma 6 from
(
Richtárik et al. 2023
)
.

Lemma 15

(Lemma 6 from
Richtárik et al. 2023
)
.

If Assumption
2
holds, then for
i\in[n]
, we have

\sum\limits_{j:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}((\nabla f_{i}(x))_{j}-(\nabla f_{i}(y))_{j})^{2}\leq L_{i}^{2}\sum\limits_{j:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}\quad\forall x\mathchar 59\relax y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}.

(3.79)

Now, we proceed to the following Lemma, which aims to provide a tighter bound for the quantity

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}\|\nabla f_{i}(x)-\nabla f_{i}(y)\|^{2}.

Lemma 16
.

If Assumption
2
holds, then

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}\|\nabla f_{i}(x)-\nabla f_{i}(y)\|^{2}\leq c{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\|x-y\|^{2}.

(3.80)

Proof.

The proof commences as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}\|\nabla f_{i}(x)-\nabla f_{i}(y)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}\sum\limits_{j:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}((\nabla f_{i}(x))_{j}-(\nabla f_{i}(y))_{j})^{2}

(3.81)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_smoothness}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}L_{i}^{2}\sum\limits_{j:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum\limits_{j:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}\left[(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}\right].

To advance our derivations, we consider the maximum value over
\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\dfrac{1}{L_{i}}\|\nabla f_{i}(x)-\nabla f_{i}(y)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_aux_5}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}\left[(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}\left[(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}\max\limits_{j\in[d]}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\max\limits_{j\in[d]}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}\right]\sum\limits_{j=1}^{d}(x_{j}-y_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\max\limits_{j\in[d]}\sum\limits_{i:(i\mathchar 59\relax j)\notin\mathcal{Z}}L_{i}\right]\|x-y\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\max\limits_{j\in[d]}\sum\limits_{i\in{\cal I}_{j}}L_{i}\right]\|x-y\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{ch3:eq:sparse_c_definiton}}{=}

\displaystyle c{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L_{\rm AM}}\|x-y\|^{2}\mathchar 59\relax

what completes the proof.
∎

For clarity and easy reference, we recapitulate Lemma 10 from
Richtárik et al. 2023
.

Lemma 17

(Lemma 10 from
Richtárik et al. 2023
)
.

The iterates of Algorithm
7
method satisfy
